=== TABLE 393 ===
Risk | Why it matters | Mitigation (where handled)
Plan drift | individual results deviate from the locked plan | Lock the plan (Lab 3/7); validation rejects any deviation before the human sees it.
Ungrounded output | content not backed by verified skills and stated role requirements | Grounded generation with an evidence record per unit (Lab 9); ungrounded items are rejected.
Failed checks slip through | a result violates a rule (recommendations cite the specific skill gaps) | Validation is a hard gate (Lab 10/11); failing results are diagnosed and regenerated, never advanced.
Runaway loops | the regenerate loop never converges | Bound MAX_ATTEMPTS (Lab 7/10); on exhaustion, escalate to the placement officer / mentor with a diagnosis.
Over-automation | the system acts without the placement officer / mentor | The human gate is a hard stop; all outputs are drafts; edits and overrides are effortless and logged (Lab 12).

=== TABLE 397 ===
Phase | What it establishes
Phase A — Foundations (Labs 1–6) | Turn a single agent loop into a tool-using, skill-driven, memory-backed agent that reaches all data through one connector and runs inside a governed runtime with an audit log.
Phase B — Orchestration (Labs 7–8) | Wire the specialist agents into an explicit node graph, then parallelise the per-unit work and merge the best results.
Phase C — Applied reliability (Labs 9–11) | Ground every output in evidence, make the pipeline self-healing on validation failure, and treat the spec as a tested build.
Phase D — Governance & capstone (Labs 12–14) | Add safety, approval, evaluation and rollback; plug in domain specialists; integrate everything into a deployed, team-owned system.

The | The Application Processing Agent compiles the application, documents, and offer/NOC once an eligible match is confirmed.
The | The Approval Agent routes the application to the Faculty Mentor, HoD, and Internship Cell before the student is released.
The | The Progress Tracking Agent collects periodic reports, mentor check-ins, and attendance/stipend confirmation through the internship.
The | The Evaluation Agent compiles employer feedback, mentor assessment, and reports into a final evaluation; mentor/Internship Cell/HoD reviews and certifies the grade.


=== TABLE 399 ===
# | Agent | Role & communication | Guardrails
0 | apniLeap AURA Coordinator | Receives the faculty/HoD prompt command, checks permissions, creates the run plan, initializes agents, and monitors state. | Never bypass human approval; enforce data scope, audit logging, source citation, and exception routing.
1 | Opportunity Discovery Agent | Surfaces institution-approved internship opportunities matched to each student, in parallel with B. | Surface only verified postings; never rank an unverified company above approved ones; cite source and date.
2 | Eligibility Verification Agent | Verifies CGPA, backlog, and credit eligibility against current-year norms, in parallel with A. | Use only the approved rule set; never waive a criterion automatically; flag borderline cases for review.
3 | Application Processing Agent | Compiles the application, resume, and required documents for a confirmed, eligible match. | Do not submit for an ineligible student; verify all documents; flag incomplete submissions.
4 | Approval Agent | Routes the application to Faculty Mentor, HoD, and Internship Cell for approval. | Never release a student without all three approvals recorded; keep a timestamped trail; route rejections with the reason.
5 | Progress Tracking Agent | Collects periodic reports, check-ins, and attendance/stipend confirmation through the semester. | Flag missed check-ins rather than assuming completion; never alter a submitted report; escalate prolonged non-reporting.
6 | Evaluation Agent | Compiles employer feedback, mentor assessment, and reports into a final evaluation and proposed grade. | Base the grade only on verifiable evidence; show weightage and source; never finalize without mentor/HoD sign-off.
7 | Faculty Mentor / Internship Cell / HoD Review | Reviews exceptions across eligibility, approval, progress, and evaluation; certifies the final grade. | Mentor/Internship Cell/HoD decision is the final authority for certifying completion and grade.

=== TABLE 402 ===
function run_internship_application_tracking_portal(request):
    ctx = Coordinator.new_run(request)        # isolated, checkpointed
    plan = Coordinator.build_plan(request)     # locked before work begins
    ctx = OpportunityDiscoveryAgent.step(ctx, plan)
    ctx = EligibilityVerificationAgent.step(ctx, plan)
    ctx = ApplicationProcessingAgent.step(ctx, plan)
    ctx = ApprovalAgent.step(ctx, plan)
    ctx = ProgressTrackingAgent.step(ctx, plan)
    ctx = EvaluationAgent.step(ctx, plan)
    ctx = FacultyMentorInternshipCellHoDReview.step(ctx, plan)
    report = ValidationAgent.validate(ctx, plan)   # hard gate
    while not report.passed and attempts < MAX:    # self-healing (Lab 10)
        Coordinator.regenerate(report.failing); report = validate(ctx, plan)
    draft = assemble(ctx)                          # the tracked internship application
    decision = TheGate.review(draft)  # HUMAN — final authority
    if decision.approved:
        final = apply(decision.edits, draft)
        Governance.publish(final, version=next()); Audit.log_all()
    return final


=== TABLE 403 ===
function ValidationAgent.validate(ctx, plan):
    checks = [
      ('deadlines are tracked accurately', test_0(ctx, plan)),
      ('eligibility is enforced', test_1(ctx, plan)),
      ('no submission happens without student approval', test_2(ctx, plan)),
      ('status changes are audited', test_3(ctx, plan)),
    ]
    failing = [n for n,ok in checks if not ok]
    return Report(passed = not failing, failing = failing,
                  diagnosis = explain(failing))


=== TABLE 404 ===
connector.expose:
    read('opportunity listings')
    read('student profiles')
    read('the application-status feed')
agents.use(connector)   # no agent touches a source directly


=== TABLE 405 ===
Stage | What happens
Plan / slot | The coordinator locks the plan and picks one application to work — its constraints fixed before any content is chosen.
Source candidate | A retrieval agent proposes a candidate for this application from opportunity listings, with provenance.
Grounded candidate | A generation agent produces a fresh candidate grounded in the opportunity's eligibility criteria, attaching an evidence record (source, scope, tags).
Screen & select | Candidates are calibrated and screened; the best-aligned one that satisfies the plan is selected, the rest logged.
Validation | The result is checked (deadlines are tracked accurately; no submission happens without student approval); on failure only this application is regenerated.
Human review | The Faculty Coordinator sees the draft tracked internship application with its evidence and validation report, and approves, edits or overrides.
Publish & audit | On approval the governed publish versions the tracked internship application, records the audit trail, and updates history for future runs.


=== TABLE 406 ===
Stage | Execution mode & coordinator behavior
Gate 1: eligibility window | Sequential — start only after student profiles and current-year criteria are loaded.
Parallel discovery & eligibility | Parallel — Opportunity Discovery surfaces matches while Eligibility Verification checks status.
Application join | Sequential — Application Processing runs once a student has both an eligible status and a matched opportunity.
Approval gate | Sequential — Mentor, HoD, and Internship Cell sign-off is required before release.
Progress tracking | Sequential, ongoing — runs continuously through the semester, independent of other students.
Evaluation | Sequential — the final grade is compiled only after the tracked period ends.
Dashboard update | Parallel after approval — tracking dashboard, progress logs, and evaluation status publish together.


=== TABLE 407 ===
Panel | Shows
Opportunity matches | Ranked, verified postings per student.
Eligibility & approvals | Eligibility status and the timestamped mentor/HoD/Internship-Cell trail.
Progress logs | Periodic reports, check-ins, and flagged gaps through the semester.
Final evaluation | Employer feedback, mentor assessment, and certified grade.

6 | Recommendation Agent | Drafts an evidence-backed action plan for each flagged student. | Base plans only on flagged signals and approved intervention types; never contact a student; route through a mentor.
7 | Faculty Mentor / HoD / Dean Review | Reviews flagged students and actions, approves or adjusts the plan, logs the outcome; HoD/Dean review trends. | Mentor/HoD/Dean decision is the final authority for contacting a student or logging an intervention.

=== TABLE 446 ===
function run_student_success_early_warning_system(request):
    ctx = Coordinator.new_run(request)        # isolated, checkpointed
    plan = Coordinator.build_plan(request)     # locked before work begins
    ctx = ContineoDataCollectionAgent.step(ctx, plan)
    ctx = LMSDataCollectionAgent.step(ctx, plan)
    ctx = InternshipDataCollectionAgent.step(ctx, plan)
    ctx = PlacementDataCollectionAgent.step(ctx, plan)
    ctx = RiskAnalysisAgent.step(ctx, plan)
    ctx = RecommendationAgent.step(ctx, plan)
    ctx = FacultyMentorHoDDeanReview.step(ctx, plan)
    report = ValidationAgent.validate(ctx, plan)   # hard gate
    while not report.passed and attempts < MAX:    # self-healing (Lab 10)
        Coordinator.regenerate(report.failing); report = validate(ctx, plan)
    draft = assemble(ctx)                          # the early-warning alert and intervention plan
    decision = TheGate.review(draft)  # HUMAN — final authority
    if decision.approved:
        final = apply(decision.edits, draft)
        Governance.publish(final, version=next()); Audit.log_all()
    return final


=== TABLE 447 ===
function ValidationAgent.validate(ctx, plan):
    checks = [
      ('each alert cites its triggering signals', test_0(ctx, plan)),
      ('thresholds are faculty-set', test_1(ctx, plan)),
      ('no student is labelled without advisor review', test_2(ctx, plan)),
      ('the privacy scope is enforced', test_3(ctx, plan)),
    ]
    failing = [n for n,ok in checks if not ok]
    return Report(passed = not failing, failing = failing,
                  diagnosis = explain(failing))

=== TABLE 449 ===
Stage | What happens
Plan / slot | The coordinator locks the plan and picks one student to work — its constraints fixed before any content is chosen.
Source candidate | A retrieval agent proposes a candidate for this student from attendance & assessment data, with provenance.
Grounded candidate | A generation agent produces a fresh candidate grounded in verified academic records and faculty-set thresholds, attaching an evidence record (source, scope, tags).
Screen & select | Candidates are calibrated and screened; the best-aligned one that satisfies the plan is selected, the rest logged.
Validation | The result is checked (each alert cites its triggering signals; no student is labelled without advisor review); on failure only this student is regenerated.
Human review | The Mentor / Academic Advisor sees the draft early-warning alert and intervention plan with its evidence and validation report, and approves, edits or overrides.
Publish & audit | On approval the governed publish versions the early-warning alert and intervention plan, records the audit trail, and updates history for future runs.


=== TABLE 450 ===
Stage | Execution mode & coordinator behavior
Gate 1: data intake | Sequential — start only after all four sources are linked and authorized for the semester.
Parallel collection | Parallel — Contineo, LMS, Internship, and Placement agents each pull records at the same time.
Risk scoring | Sequential — Risk Analysis Agent computes the composite score after all four return data.
Recommendation | Sequential — action plans are drafted only for flagged students.
Mentor approval | Sequential — each recommended action is approved or adjusted before it is logged.
Dashboard update | Parallel after approval — early-warning dashboard, risk scores, and intervention log publish together.


=== TABLE 451 ===
Panel | Shows
Composite risk view | Per-student risk score with contributing signals.
At-risk flags | Flagged students with visible reasoning and review path.
Action plans | Drafted interventions awaiting mentor approval.
Intervention log | Approved actions and outcomes, with aggregate risk trends.

=== TABLE 480 ===
Order | Capability | What it delivers | Agents involved
1 | Signal collection | Normalize academic, engagement, internship, placement data. | Coordinator, Data Collection Agents.

3 | Recommendation drafting | Evidence-backed action plans for flagged students. | Recommendation Agent.
4 | Mentor approval | Review, adjust, and log interventions. | Faculty Mentor Review.
5 | Trend review | Aggregate risk trends and outcomes per cycle. | HoD/Dean Review.
6 | Early-warning dashboard | Risk view, flags, and intervention log in one place. | Coordinator.


=== TABLE 481 ===
Risk | Why it matters | Mitigation (where handled)
Plan drift | individual results deviate from the locked plan | Lock the plan (Lab 3/7); validation rejects any deviation before the human sees it.
Ungrounded output | content not backed by verified academic records and faculty-set thresholds | Grounded generation with an evidence record per unit (Lab 9); ungrounded items are rejected.
Failed checks slip through | a result violates a rule (each alert cites its triggering signals) | Validation is a hard gate (Lab 10/11); failing results are diagnosed and regenerated, never advanced.
Runaway loops | the regenerate loop never converges | Bound MAX_ATTEMPTS (Lab 7/10); on exhaustion, escalate to the mentor / academic advisor with a diagnosis.
Over-automation | the system acts without the mentor / academic advisor | The human gate is a hard stop; all outputs are drafts; edits and overrides are effortless and logged (Lab 12).


=== TABLE 482 ===
Week | Labs | Focus | Exit deliverable
Week 1 | Labs 1–3 | Planner loop, tools, reusable skills | Runnable planner + core skill
Week 2 | Labs 4–6 | Memory/retrieval, connector, governed runtime | Data via connector; audited runs
Week 3 | Labs 7–8 | Full node graph + parallel processing | End-to-end draft — shared with faculty
Week 4 | Labs 9–10 | Grounding + self-healing validation | Grounded output; auto-repair on faults
Week 5 | Labs 11–12 | Tested build + safety/governance | Tested artifact; approval, metrics, rollback
Week 6 | Labs 13–14 | Domain specialisation + capstone | Deployed system + demo on a real case


=== TABLE 483 ===
Checkpoint:  from the 3rd week, worklets are shared with all faculty for feedback — plan the Week-3 end-to-end draft (Labs 7–8) to be demo-ready.


=== TABLE 484 ===
Term | Meaning
Coordinator | The orchestrator agent that owns run-state, plan, budgets and the audit log.
Node graph | The explicit wiring of agents, gates and back-edges for one worklet.
Human gate | The approval step; the named human is the final authority.
Grounding | Linking an output to a real in-scope source (evidence).
Validation | The automated acceptance gate a result must pass before human review.
Blueprint / plan | The locked specification the pipeline obeys.


=== TABLE 485 ===
Phase | What it establishes
Phase A — Foundations (Labs 1–6) | Turn a single agent loop into a tool-using, skill-driven, memory-backed agent that reaches all data through one connector and runs inside a governed runtime with an audit log.
Phase B — Orchestration (Labs 7–8) | Wire the specialist agents into an explicit node graph, then parallelise the per-unit work and merge the best results.
Phase C — Applied reliability (Labs 9–11) | Ground every output in evidence, make the pipeline self-healing on validation failure, and treat the spec as a tested build.
Phase D — Governance & capstone (Labs 12–14) | Add safety, approval, evaluation and rollback; plug in domain specialists; integrate everything into a deployed, team-owned system.


=== TABLE 486 ===
Capability | What it means
Faculty | Faculty states the research question, target venue, collaborators, and deadline.
The | The Literature Discovery Agent searches scholarly metadata sources and returns a ranked reading list with summaries.
The | The Gap & Idea Analysis Agent compares the stated question against discovered literature and flags what is genuinely novel.
The | The Drafting & Writing Agent produces an outline first, then a full draft only after faculty confirms the outline.
The | The Citation & Compliance Agent checks formatting, reference completeness, and venue-specific requirements.
The | The Funding/Journal Radar Agent runs independently, surfacing upcoming deadlines relevant to faculty's interests.


=== TABLE 487 ===
# | Agent | Role & communication | Guardrails
0 | apniLeap AURA Coordinator | Receives the research request, creates the run plan, initializes agents with scoped instructions, monitors state. | Never bypass faculty approval; enforce source citation and audit logging.
1 | Literature Discovery Agent | Searches scholarly metadata sources and returns a ranked, summarized reading list. | Use only verifiable scholarly sources; never fabricate a citation or abstract.
2 | Gap & Idea Analysis Agent | Compares the research question against discovered literature and flags novelty versus overlap. | State uncertainty explicitly rather than asserting novelty it cannot verify.
3 | Drafting & Writing Agent | Produces an outline, then a full draft in the target venue's structure. | Draft only; mark AI-authored text clearly; no submission without faculty approval.
4 | Citation & Compliance Agent | Checks formatting, reference completeness, and venue-specific requirements. | Flag missing or inconsistent citations rather than inventing a reference.
5 | Funding / Journal Radar Agent | Continuously surfaces relevant journals, calls for papers, and funding deadlines. | Only report from verifiable sources; timestamp every alert.
6 | Collaboration Agent | Tracks co-author section ownership and consolidates edits into one version. | Preserve author attribution; never silently overwrite a co-author's edit.
7 | Faculty Review | Reviews exceptions, approves drafts, requests corrections or scope changes. | Faculty decision is final authority for all submissions.
8 | Export & Repository Agent | Produces the final formatted document and logs it in the research tracker. | Keep version history and source links; label draft versus approved status.


=== TABLE 488 ===
From | To | Message / payload | When
Coordinator | Literature Discovery Agent | run-context + task | run start
Literature Discovery Agent | Gap & Idea Analysis Agent | state / partial result | in pipeline
Gap & Idea Analysis Agent | Drafting & Writing Agent | state / partial result | in pipeline
Drafting & Writing Agent | Citation & Compliance Agent | state / partial result | in pipeline
Citation & Compliance Agent | Funding / Journal Radar Agent | state / partial result | in pipeline
Funding / Journal Radar Agent | Collaboration Agent | state / partial result | in pipeline
Collaboration Agent | Faculty Review | state / partial result | in pipeline
Faculty Review | Export & Repository Agent | state / partial result | in pipeline
Export & Repository Agent | The Researcher gate | draft research brief with cited evidence + evidence | for review
The Researcher gate | Coordinator | approve | edit | override | human decision
Coordinator | Governance / Publish | final research brief with cited evidence (versioned) | on approval


=== TABLE 489 ===
Entity | Key fields
Research question / topic | Problem area, keywords, and scope for the current task.
Target venue | Journal, conference, or funding call, with citation style and length limits.
Prior work library | Faculty's past papers and citation library for style and self-citation consistency.
Collaborator list | Co-authors and their section ownership, if multi-author.
Deadline | Submission or funding-call deadline to track and alert against.


=== TABLE 490 ===
function run_research_assistant_agent(request):
    ctx = Coordinator.new_run(request)        # isolated, checkpointed
    plan = Coordinator.build_plan(request)     # locked before work begins
    ctx = LiteratureDiscoveryAgent.step(ctx, plan)
    ctx = GapIdeaAnalysisAgent.step(ctx, plan)
    ctx = DraftingWritingAgent.step(ctx, plan)
    ctx = CitationComplianceAgent.step(ctx, plan)
    ctx = FundingJournalRadarAgent.step(ctx, plan)
    ctx = CollaborationAgent.step(ctx, plan)
    ctx = FacultyReview.step(ctx, plan)
    ctx = ExportRepositoryAgent.step(ctx, plan)
    report = ValidationAgent.validate(ctx, plan)   # hard gate
    while not report.passed and attempts < MAX:    # self-healing (Lab 10)
        Coordinator.regenerate(report.failing); report = validate(ctx, plan)
    draft = assemble(ctx)                          # the research brief with cited evidence
    decision = TheGate.review(draft)  # HUMAN — final authority
    if decision.approved:
        final = apply(decision.edits, draft)
        Governance.publish(final, version=next()); Audit.log_all()
    return final


=== TABLE 491 ===
function ValidationAgent.validate(ctx, plan):
    checks = [
      ('every claim cites a real source', test_0(ctx, plan)),
      ('no reference is fabricated', test_1(ctx, plan)),
      ('scope stays within approved corpora', test_2(ctx, plan)),
      ('the draft is marked assistant-generated', test_3(ctx, plan)),
    ]
    failing = [n for n,ok in checks if not ok]
    return Report(passed = not failing, failing = failing,
                  diagnosis = explain(failing))

    read('notes & drafts')

=== TABLE 493 ===
Stage | What happens
Plan / slot | The coordinator locks the plan and picks one query / topic to work — its constraints fixed before any content is chosen.
Source candidate | A retrieval agent proposes a candidate for this query / topic from the paper corpus & databases, with provenance.
Grounded candidate | A generation agent produces a fresh candidate grounded in retrieved papers and primary sources, attaching an evidence record (source, scope, tags).
Screen & select | Candidates are calibrated and screened; the best-aligned one that satisfies the plan is selected, the rest logged.
Validation | The result is checked (every claim cites a real source; scope stays within approved corpora); on failure only this query / topic is regenerated.
Human review | The Researcher sees the draft research brief with cited evidence with its evidence and validation report, and approves, edits or overrides.
Publish & audit | On approval the governed publish versions the research brief with cited evidence, records the audit trail, and updates history for future runs.


=== TABLE 494 ===
Stage | Execution mode & coordinator behavior
Gate 1: intent and venue | Sequential — drafting starts only after faculty confirms topic, venue, and deadline.
Parallel discovery | Parallel — Literature Discovery Agent and Funding/Journal Radar Agent run independently.
Join and drafting | Sequential — the Drafting Agent starts only once the gap analysis is available.
Parallel compliance | Parallel — Citation & Compliance Agent and Collaboration Agent both work once a draft exists.
Faculty approval | Sequential — faculty approves before export or submission.
Export and tracking | Parallel after approval — export and repository logging run together.

Plan drift | individual results deviate from the locked plan | Lock the plan (Lab 3/7); validation rejects any deviation before the human sees it.
Ungrounded output | content not backed by retrieved papers and primary sources | Grounded generation with an evidence record per unit (Lab 9); ungrounded items are rejected.
Failed checks slip through | a result violates a rule (every claim cites a real source) | Validation is a hard gate (Lab 10/11); failing results are diagnosed and regenerated, never advanced.
Runaway loops | the regenerate loop never converges | Bound MAX_ATTEMPTS (Lab 7/10); on exhaustion, escalate to the researcher with a diagnosis.
Over-automation | the system acts without the researcher | The human gate is a hard stop; all outputs are drafts; edits and overrides are effortless and logged (Lab 12).


=== TABLE 526 ===
Week | Labs | Focus | Exit deliverable
Week 1 | Labs 1–3 | Planner loop, tools, reusable skills | Runnable planner + core skill
Week 2 | Labs 4–6 | Memory/retrieval, connector, governed runtime | Data via connector; audited runs
Week 3 | Labs 7–8 | Full node graph + parallel processing | End-to-end draft — shared with faculty
Week 4 | Labs 9–10 | Grounding + self-healing validation | Grounded output; auto-repair on faults
Week 5 | Labs 11–12 | Tested build + safety/governance | Tested artifact; approval, metrics, rollback
Week 6 | Labs 13–14 | Domain specialisation + capstone | Deployed system + demo on a real case


=== TABLE 527 ===
Checkpoint:  from the 3rd week, worklets are shared with all faculty for feedback — plan the Week-3 end-to-end draft (Labs 7–8) to be demo-ready.


=== TABLE 528 ===
Term | Meaning
Coordinator | The orchestrator agent that owns run-state, plan, budgets and the audit log.
Node graph | The explicit wiring of agents, gates and back-edges for one worklet.
Human gate | The approval step; the named human is the final authority.
Grounding | Linking an output to a real in-scope source (evidence).
Validation | The automated acceptance gate a result must pass before human review.
Blueprint / plan | The locked specification the pipeline obeys.


=== TABLE 529 ===
Phase | What it establishes
Phase A — Foundations (Labs 1–6) | Turn a single agent loop into a tool-using, skill-driven, memory-backed agent that reaches all data through one connector and runs inside a governed runtime with an audit log.
Phase B — Orchestration (Labs 7–8) | Wire the specialist agents into an explicit node graph, then parallelise the per-unit work and merge the best results.
Phase C — Applied reliability (Labs 9–11) | Ground every output in evidence, make the pipeline self-healing on validation failure, and treat the spec as a tested build.
Phase D — Governance & capstone (Labs 12–14) | Add safety, approval, evaluation and rollback; plug in domain specialists; integrate everything into a deployed, team-owned system.


=== TABLE 530 ===
Capability | What it means
Faculty | Faculty specifies their research area, target funding types, and consent to use their profile/publication history for matching.
The | The Funding Discovery Agent surfaces matched calls while the Literature Review Agent compiles a related-work and novelty summary, in parallel.
The | The Proposal Drafting Agent drafts the narrative using the matched call's requirements and the literature summary.
The | The Budget Planning Agent prepares an agency-compliant budget while the Compliance Checking Agent runs an eligibility/format check, in parallel.
The | The Submission Agent packages the proposal, budget, and compliance checklist into the agency's required format.
Dean | Dean R&D/HoD/Research Office reviews the package, approves or requests corrections, and authorizes final submission.


=== TABLE 531 ===
# | Agent | Role & communication | Guardrails
0 | apniLeap AURA Coordinator | Receives the faculty/Dean R&D prompt command, checks permissions, creates the run plan, initializes agents, monitors state. | Never bypass human approval; enforce data scope, audit logging, source citation, and exception routing.
1 | Funding Discovery Agent | Surfaces funding calls (DST, SERB, AICTE, industry CSR) matched to area, eligibility, and deadline, in parallel with B. | Surface only verified agencies; never present an expired call as active; cite source and deadline.
2 | Literature Review Agent | Compiles related work and a novelty/gap summary from approved databases, in parallel with A. | Cite every claim to a verifiable source; never fabricate a reference; flag thin literature rather than overstating novelty.
3 | Proposal Drafting Agent | Drafts objectives, methodology, and timeline using the call requirements and literature summary. | Draft within provided scope; mark AI text as a draft; never present preliminary findings as established.
4 | Budget Planning Agent | Prepares an itemized budget per agency norms and ceilings, in parallel with E. | Use approved templates/ceilings; flag line items exceeding norms; never adjust figures without showing the basis.
5 | Compliance Checking Agent | Checks eligibility, formatting, and documentation against the agency's guidelines, in parallel with D. | Check against the specific call's guidelines; never mark compliant without evidence; flag ambiguous requirements.
6 | Submission Agent | Packages the proposal, budget, and checklist into the agency's required format. | Do not submit without Dean R&D/HoD/Research Office sign-off; keep a version-controlled package; re-trigger review on any post-sign-off change.
7 | Dean R&D / HoD / Research Office Review | Reviews the package, approves or requests corrections, and authorizes final submission. | Dean R&D/HoD/Research Office decision is the final authority for authorizing submission.

Compliance Checking Agent | Submission Agent | state / partial result | in pipeline

Dean R&D / HoD / Research Office Review | The Pi / Research Office gate | draft grant proposal and compliance package + evidence | for review

=== TABLE 534 ===
function run_research_grant_management_system(request):
    ctx = Coordinator.new_run(request)        # isolated, checkpointed
    plan = Coordinator.build_plan(request)     # locked before work begins
    ctx = FundingDiscoveryAgent.step(ctx, plan)
    ctx = LiteratureReviewAgent.step(ctx, plan)
    ctx = ProposalDraftingAgent.step(ctx, plan)
    ctx = BudgetPlanningAgent.step(ctx, plan)
    ctx = ComplianceCheckingAgent.step(ctx, plan)
    ctx = SubmissionAgent.step(ctx, plan)
    ctx = DeanRDHoDResearchOfficeReview.step(ctx, plan)
    report = ValidationAgent.validate(ctx, plan)   # hard gate
    while not report.passed and attempts < MAX:    # self-healing (Lab 10)
        Coordinator.regenerate(report.failing); report = validate(ctx, plan)
    draft = assemble(ctx)                          # the grant proposal and compliance package
    decision = TheGate.review(draft)  # HUMAN — final authority
    if decision.approved:
        final = apply(decision.edits, draft)
        Governance.publish(final, version=next()); Audit.log_all()
    return final


=== TABLE 535 ===
function ValidationAgent.validate(ctx, plan):
    checks = [
      ('budget totals reconcile', test_0(ctx, plan)),
      ('eligibility and compliance are met', test_1(ctx, plan)),
      ('no submission happens without PI approval', test_2(ctx, plan)),
      ('every deadline is tracked', test_3(ctx, plan)),
    ]
    failing = [n for n,ok in checks if not ok]
    return Report(passed = not failing, failing = failing,
                  diagnosis = explain(failing))

    read('budget & personnel data')
    read('compliance rules')
agents.use(connector)   # no agent touches a source directly


=== TABLE 537 ===
Stage | What happens
Plan / slot | The coordinator locks the plan and picks one proposal / grant to work — its constraints fixed before any content is chosen.
Source candidate | A retrieval agent proposes a candidate for this proposal / grant from funding calls & guidelines, with provenance.
Grounded candidate | A generation agent produces a fresh candidate grounded in the funder's guidelines and institutional policy, attaching an evidence record (source, scope, tags).
Screen & select | Candidates are calibrated and screened; the best-aligned one that satisfies the plan is selected, the rest logged.
Validation | The result is checked (budget totals reconcile; no submission happens without PI approval); on failure only this proposal / grant is regenerated.
Human review | The Pi / Research Office sees the draft grant proposal and compliance package with its evidence and validation report, and approves, edits or overrides.
Publish & audit | On approval the governed publish versions the grant proposal and compliance package, records the audit trail, and updates history for future runs.


=== TABLE 538 ===
Stage | Execution mode & coordinator behavior
Gate 1: profile intake | Sequential — start only after research area, funding type, and consent are provided.
Parallel discovery & review | Parallel — Funding Discovery surfaces calls while Literature Review compiles the novelty summary.
Drafting | Sequential — drafting runs after both the call match and literature summary are available.
Parallel budget & compliance | Parallel — Budget Planning and Compliance Checking both run against the drafted proposal.
Packaging | Sequential — the Submission Agent assembles the package after budget and compliance are available.
Stakeholder approval | Sequential — Dean R&D/HoD/Research Office approves and authorizes; corrections route back.
Dashboard update | Parallel after approval — grants dashboard (pipeline, deadlines, compliance) publishes once approved.


=== TABLE 539 ===
Panel | Shows
Funding pipeline | Matched calls, deadlines, and proposal status.
Proposal & literature | Draft narrative and novelty summary with citations.
Budget & compliance | Itemized budget against ceilings and the compliance checklist.

=== TABLE 575 ===
# | Agent | Role & communication | Guardrails
0 | apniLeap AURA Coordinator | Receives the faculty/HoD prompt command, checks permissions, creates the run plan, initializes agents, monitors state. | Never bypass human approval; enforce data scope, audit logging, source citation, and exception routing.
1 | Faculty Profile Agent | Normalizes faculty identity, department, and existing publication list into a structured profile. | Validate consent before pulling data; never expose unpublished/under-review work beyond permissions; flag incomplete profiles.
2 | Google Scholar Harvest Agent | Harvests publications, co-authors, and citation counts from the linked Scholar profile. | Pull only from the verified profile; never scrape unrelated profiles; cite source URL and retrieval date.
3 | Scopus/WoS Verification Agent | Cross-checks each harvested publication against Scopus and WoS indexing and metadata. | Use only licensed API access; never fabricate an indexing status; flag mismatches for human review.
4 | Duplicate Detection Agent | Detects near-duplicate and cross-source records and proposes merges with an audit trail. | Never auto-delete; flag and merge only with reviewer confirmation; preserve links to each source.
5 | Citation Analytics Agent | Computes h-index, total citations, and multi-year trends from the deduplicated set. | Calculate only from verified, de-duplicated records; show method and source; do not overstate confidence on incomplete data.
6 | Dashboard Agent | Assembles the publication dashboard and exportable NIRF/NAAC reports from approved analytics. | Publish only Research-Office-approved data; keep raw harvested data separate; version every report export.
7 | Validation Agent | Cross-checks completeness and consistency across all outputs before human review. | Never certify a report missing required citations; route exceptions to the Research Office; do not suppress inconsistencies.
8 | HoD / Dean / Research Office Review | Reviews exceptions and low-confidence matches, approves analytics and reports, or sends corrections back. | HoD/Dean/Research Office decision is the final authority for institution-facing metrics and NIRF/NAAC submissions.

=== TABLE 578 ===
function run_research_intelligence_publication_analyt(request):
    ctx = Coordinator.new_run(request)        # isolated, checkpointed
    plan = Coordinator.build_plan(request)     # locked before work begins
    ctx = FacultyProfileAgent.step(ctx, plan)
    ctx = GoogleScholarHarvestAgent.step(ctx, plan)
    ctx = ScopusWoSVerificationAgent.step(ctx, plan)
    ctx = DuplicateDetectionAgent.step(ctx, plan)
    ctx = CitationAnalyticsAgent.step(ctx, plan)
    ctx = DashboardAgent.step(ctx, plan)
    ctx = ValidationAgent.step(ctx, plan)
    ctx = HoDDeanResearchOfficeReview.step(ctx, plan)
    report = ValidationAgent.validate(ctx, plan)   # hard gate
    while not report.passed and attempts < MAX:    # self-healing (Lab 10)
        Coordinator.regenerate(report.failing); report = validate(ctx, plan)
    draft = assemble(ctx)                          # the publication analytics report
    decision = TheGate.review(draft)  # HUMAN — final authority
    if decision.approved:
        final = apply(decision.edits, draft)
        Governance.publish(final, version=next()); Audit.log_all()
    return final


=== TABLE 579 ===
function ValidationAgent.validate(ctx, plan):
    checks = [
      ('each metric cites its source', test_0(ctx, plan)),
      ('author disambiguation is verified', test_1(ctx, plan)),
      ('no ranking is published without review', test_2(ctx, plan)),
      ('metric caveats are stated', test_3(ctx, plan)),
    ]
    failing = [n for n,ok in checks if not ok]
    return Report(passed = not failing, failing = failing,
                  diagnosis = explain(failing))

Screen & select | Candidates are calibrated and screened; the best-aligned one that satisfies the plan is selected, the rest logged.
Validation | The result is checked (each metric cites its source; no ranking is published without review); on failure only this researcher / unit is regenerated.

=== TABLE 612 ===
Order | Capability | What it delivers | Agents involved

=== TABLE 613 ===
Risk | Why it matters | Mitigation (where handled)
Plan drift | individual results deviate from the locked plan | Lock the plan (Lab 3/7); validation rejects any deviation before the human sees it.
Ungrounded output | content not backed by verified bibliometric records | Grounded generation with an evidence record per unit (Lab 9); ungrounded items are rejected.
Failed checks slip through | a result violates a rule (each metric cites its source) | Validation is a hard gate (Lab 10/11); failing results are diagnosed and regenerated, never advanced.
Runaway loops | the regenerate loop never converges | Bound MAX_ATTEMPTS (Lab 7/10); on exhaustion, escalate to the HoD / research dean with a diagnosis.
Over-automation | the system acts without the HoD / research dean | The human gate is a hard stop; all outputs are drafts; edits and overrides are effortless and logged (Lab 12).

Week 2 | Labs 4–6 | Memory/retrieval, connector, governed runtime | Data via connector; audited runs
Week 3 | Labs 7–8 | Full node graph + parallel processing | End-to-end draft — shared with faculty
Week 4 | Labs 9–10 | Grounding + self-healing validation | Grounded output; auto-repair on faults
Week 5 | Labs 11–12 | Tested build + safety/governance | Tested artifact; approval, metrics, rollback
Week 6 | Labs 13–14 | Domain specialisation + capstone | Deployed system + demo on a real case

=== TABLE 616 ===
Term | Meaning
Coordinator | The orchestrator agent that owns run-state, plan, budgets and the audit log.
Node graph | The explicit wiring of agents, gates and back-edges for one worklet.
Human gate | The approval step; the named human is the final authority.
Grounding | Linking an output to a real in-scope source (evidence).
Validation | The automated acceptance gate a result must pass before human review.
Blueprint / plan | The locked specification the pipeline obeys.


=== TABLE 617 ===
Phase | What it establishes
Phase A — Foundations (Labs 1–6) | Turn a single agent loop into a tool-using, skill-driven, memory-backed agent that reaches all data through one connector and runs inside a governed runtime with an audit log.
Phase B — Orchestration (Labs 7–8) | Wire the specialist agents into an explicit node graph, then parallelise the per-unit work and merge the best results.
Phase C — Applied reliability (Labs 9–11) | Ground every output in evidence, make the pipeline self-healing on validation failure, and treat the spec as a tested build.
Phase D — Governance & capstone (Labs 12–14) | Add safety, approval, evaluation and rollback; plug in domain specialists; integrate everything into a deployed, team-owned system.


=== TABLE 618 ===
Capability | What it means
Each | Each department's academic, research, placement, and finance systems are linked with institution-level read access authorized by the Registrar.
The | The Academic, Research, Placement, and Finance agents each aggregate their domain's metrics across departments, in parallel.
The | The KPI Agent synthesizes the four domain views into institution-wide KPIs aligned to NIRF/NAAC and strategic-plan targets.
The | The Report Generation Agent compiles a leadership-ready report with drill-down evidence back to the source department and metric.
Principal/Director/Registrar | Principal/Director/Registrar reviews the report, flags any KPI needing clarification, and approves it for circulation.
The | The Governing Council receives the approved report at its scheduled review cycle.


=== TABLE 619 ===
# | Agent | Role & communication | Guardrails
0 | apniLeap AURA Coordinator | Receives the leadership/Registrar prompt command, checks permissions, creates the run plan, initializes agents, monitors state. | Never bypass human approval; enforce data scope, audit logging, source citation, and exception routing.
1 | Academic Agent | Aggregates pass percentage, CO/PO attainment, and curriculum compliance across departments, in parallel with B, C, D. | Pull only from approved records; show the underlying spread; flag departments with stale or missing data.
2 | Research Agent | Aggregates publications, citations, and grants across departments, in parallel with A, C, D. | Pull only from validated records; never double-count a cross-department collaboration; cite the source system.
3 | Placement Agent | Aggregates placement percentage, average package, and internship completion, in parallel with A, B, D. | Pull only from Placement-Cell-verified records; never present a provisional offer as a confirmed placement; flag gaps.
4 | Finance Agent | Aggregates budget utilization, revenue, and grant funds at institution level, in parallel with A, B, C. | Pull only from audited/approved figures; never project a forecast as an actual; flag figures pending reconciliation.
5 | KPI Agent | Synthesizes the four domains into institution-wide KPIs aligned to NIRF/NAAC and strategic-plan targets. | Use only approved KPI definitions and targets; show formula and source; never mask a missed target in a rounded figure.
6 | Report Generation Agent | Compiles the executive report and dashboard with drill-down evidence. | Publish only approved figures; keep drill-down traceable; version every executive report.
7 | Principal / Registrar / Governing Council Review | Reviews the report and KPIs, flags items, approves for circulation; Council receives it on schedule. | Principal/Registrar/Council decision is the final authority for circulating an executive report.


=== TABLE 620 ===
From | To | Message / payload | When
Coordinator | Academic Agent | run-context + task | run start
Academic Agent | Research Agent | state / partial result | in pipeline
Research Agent | Placement Agent | state / partial result | in pipeline
Placement Agent | Finance Agent | state / partial result | in pipeline
Finance Agent | KPI Agent | state / partial result | in pipeline
KPI Agent | Report Generation Agent | state / partial result | in pipeline
Report Generation Agent | Principal / Registrar / Governing Council Review | state / partial result | in pipeline
Principal / Registrar / Governing Council Review | The Executive / Leadership gate | draft decision brief with options and risks + evidence | for review
The Executive / Leadership gate | Coordinator | approve | edit | override | human decision
Coordinator | Governance / Publish | final decision brief with options and risks (versioned) | on approval


=== TABLE 621 ===
Entity | Key fields
Domain metrics | Academic, research, placement, and finance metrics aggregated across departments.
KPI definition | Approved institution-wide KPI formulas and target values.
Target & variance | NIRF/NAAC and strategic-plan targets with computed variance.
Drill-down evidence | Traceable links from each KPI to its source department and metric.
Executive report | Leadership-ready report and dashboard, pending approval.


=== TABLE 622 ===
function run_executive_decision_support_system(request):
    ctx = Coordinator.new_run(request)        # isolated, checkpointed
    plan = Coordinator.build_plan(request)     # locked before work begins
    ctx = AcademicAgent.step(ctx, plan)
    ctx = ResearchAgent.step(ctx, plan)
    ctx = PlacementAgent.step(ctx, plan)
    ctx = FinanceAgent.step(ctx, plan)
    ctx = KPIAgent.step(ctx, plan)
    ctx = ReportGenerationAgent.step(ctx, plan)
    ctx = PrincipalRegistrarGoverningCouncilReview.step(ctx, plan)
    report = ValidationAgent.validate(ctx, plan)   # hard gate
    while not report.passed and attempts < MAX:    # self-healing (Lab 10)
        Coordinator.regenerate(report.failing); report = validate(ctx, plan)
    draft = assemble(ctx)                          # the decision brief with options and risks
    decision = TheGate.review(draft)  # HUMAN — final authority
    if decision.approved:
        final = apply(decision.edits, draft)
        Governance.publish(final, version=next()); Audit.log_all()
    return final


=== TABLE 623 ===
function ValidationAgent.validate(ctx, plan):
    checks = [
      ('every figure cites its source', test_0(ctx, plan)),
      ('assumptions are stated explicitly', test_1(ctx, plan)),
      ('each option lists its risks and trade-offs', test_2(ctx, plan)),
      ('no action is taken without leadership approval', test_3(ctx, plan)),
    ]
    failing = [n for n,ok in checks if not ok]
    return Report(passed = not failing, failing = failing,
                  diagnosis = explain(failing))


=== TABLE 624 ===
connector.expose:
    read('institutional KPIs & dashboards')
    read('department reports')
    read('external benchmarks')
agents.use(connector)   # no agent touches a source directly


=== TABLE 625 ===
Stage | What happens
Plan / slot | The coordinator locks the plan and picks one decision / KPI to work — its constraints fixed before any content is chosen.
Source candidate | A retrieval agent proposes a candidate for this decision / KPI from institutional KPIs & dashboards, with provenance.
Grounded candidate | A generation agent produces a fresh candidate grounded in verified institutional data, attaching an evidence record (source, scope, tags).
Screen & select | Candidates are calibrated and screened; the best-aligned one that satisfies the plan is selected, the rest logged.
Validation | The result is checked (every figure cites its source; each option lists its risks and trade-offs); on failure only this decision / KPI is regenerated.
Human review | The Executive / Leadership sees the draft decision brief with options and risks with its evidence and validation report, and approves, edits or overrides.
Publish & audit | On approval the governed publish versions the decision brief with options and risks, records the audit trail, and updates history for future runs.


=== TABLE 626 ===
Stage | Execution mode & coordinator behavior
Gate 1: access intake | Sequential — start only after each domain grants institution-level read access authorized by the Registrar.
Parallel domain aggregation | Parallel — Academic, Research, Placement, and Finance agents each aggregate at the same time.
KPI synthesis | Sequential — KPIs are computed only after all four domain agents return data.
Report generation | Sequential — the report is compiled after the KPI set is finalized.
Stakeholder approval | Sequential — Principal/Registrar approves before the Governing Council; corrections route back.
Dashboard update | Parallel after approval — executive dashboard (KPIs, domain metrics, report) publishes together.


=== TABLE 627 ===
Panel | Shows
Institution KPIs | KPIs against NIRF/NAAC and strategic-plan targets with variance.
Domain metrics | Academic, research, placement, and finance breakdowns.
Drill-down evidence | Traceable path from each KPI to its source department and metric.
Executive report | Approved, versioned leadership report for the Governing Council.
